31134:   function buildTree(ds){var by={},root=[];(ds||[]).forEach(function(x){by[x.id]={id:x.id,name:x.name,code:x.code,parent:x.parent_department_id,manager:x.manager_employee_id,children:[]}});Object.keys(by).forEach(function(k){var x=by[k];if(x.parent&&by[x.parent])by[x.parent].children.push(x);else root.push(x)});function node(x,depth){var manager=H.employees.filter(function(e){return e.id===x.manager})[0];return '<div class="mr-'+Math.min(depth*3,12)+' rounded-2xl border border-slate-100 p-4 bg-white shadow-sm"><div class="flex justify-between gap-3"><div><div class="font-black">'+esc(x.name)+'</div><div class="text-xs text-slate-500">'+esc(x.code||'-')+(manager?' · مدير: '+esc(manager.name):'')+'</div></div>'+badge(x.children.length+' فرعي','info')+'</div>'+(x.children.length?'<div class="mt-3 space-y-3 border-r-2 border-slate-100 pr-4">'+x.children.map(function(c){return node(c,depth+1)}).join('')+'</div>':'')+'</div>'}return root.map(function(x){return node(x,0)}).join('')||'<div class="py-10 text-center text-slate-400 font-bold">لم تُنشأ إدارات بعد</div>'}

31122:   function ppl(){return H.employees.filter(function(e){return String(e.role||'').toLowerCase()!=='owner'&&e.role!=='مالك'}).map(function(e){return{value:e.id,label:(e.name||e.email)+' — '+e.email}})}
31123:   async function loadPeople(){var d=await q('employees');H.employees=d.rows||[];return H.employees}
31124:   async function loadBranches(){var r=await supabase.from('branches').select('id,branch_code,name,is_active').eq('company_id',H.companyId).order('name');if(r.error)throw r.error;H.branches=r.data||[];return H.branches}
31125:   function branches(){return H.branches.filter(function(x){return x.is_active!==false}).map(function(x){return{value:x.id,label:(x.branch_code||'')+' — '+x.name}})}
31126:   function employeeOpts(){return ppl()}
31127:   function deptOpts(rows){return (rows||[]).map(function(x){return{value:x.id,label:x.name}})}
31128:   function posOpts(rows){return (rows||[]).map(function(x){return{value:x.id,label:x.title}})}
31129:   function scheduleOpts(rows){return (rows||[]).map(function(x){return{value:x.id,label:x.name}})}
31130:   function tabbar(){return '<div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-100 shadow-sm p-2 flex gap-2 flex-wrap">'+T.map(function(x){return '<button type="button" data-hr-tab="'+x[0]+'" class="px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl font-black text-sm '+(H.tab===x[0]?'bg-indigo-600 text-white':'text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-50')+'"><i class="fas '+x[2]+' ml-1"></i>'+x[1]+'</button>'}).join('')+'</div>'}
31131:   function employeeMeta(e){return '<div class="space-y-2 text-sm"><div><span class="text-slate-500">القسم:</span> <b>'+esc(e.department_name||e.department||'-')+'</b></div><div><span class="text-slate-500">الوظيفة:</span> <b>'+esc(e.position_name||e.job_title||e.role||'-')+'</b></div><div><span class="text-slate-500">الفرع:</span> <b>'+esc(e.branch_name||'-')+'</b></div><div><span class="text-slate-500">العقد:</span> '+(e.contract_status==='active'?badge('فعال','ok'):badge(e.contract_status||'غير موجود','muted'))+'</div></div>'}

31170:   (function installModalResilience(){
31171:     document.addEventListener('click',function(e){
31172:       var ac=e.target.closest&&e.target.closest('[data-hr-action]');
31173:       if(!ac)return;
31174:       var page=E('rw-page-container');
31175:       if(page&&page.contains(ac))return;
31176:       e.preventDefault();
31177:       handle(ac.getAttribute('data-hr-action'));
31178:     },true);
31179:     window.addEventListener('unhandledrejection',function(e){
31180:       var root=E('rw-hr-modal-root');
31181:       if(!root)return;
31182:       e.preventDefault();
31183:       var msg=e.reason&&(e.reason.message||String(e.reason));
31184:       if(msg)toast(msg,'error');
31185:     });
31186:     try{
31187:       var mo=new MutationObserver(function(){
31188:         var root=E('rw-hr-modal-root');
31189:         if(!root||!E('hr360'))return;
31190:         var f=E('rw-hr-form');
31191:         if(f&&f.lastElementChild)f.lastElementChild.style.display='none';
31192:       });
31193:       mo.observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});
31194:     }catch(e){}
31195:   }());

28805: function _exportReportCsv() {
28806:     var resultDiv = byId('report-result');
28807: 
28808:     if (!resultDiv) {
28809:         _showToast('لا يوجد تقرير للتصدير', 'info');
28810:         return;
28811:     }
28812: 
28813:     var table = resultDiv.querySelector('table');
28814: 
28815:     if (!table) {
28816:         _showToast('لا يوجد جدول قابل للتصدير', 'info');
28817:         return;
28818:     }
28819: 
28820:     var rows = table.querySelectorAll('tr');
28821:     var csv = [];
28822: 
28823:     function csvCell(value) {
28824:         var s = String(value == null ? '' : value)
28825:             .replace(/\r?\n|\r/g, ' ')
28826:             .replace(/"/g, '""');
28827: 
28828:         return '"' + s + '"';
28829:     }
28830: 
28831:     for (var i = 0; i < rows.length; i++) {
28832:         var cells = rows[i].querySelectorAll('th,td');
28833:         var line = [];
28834: 
28835:         for (var j = 0; j < cells.length; j++) {
28836:             line.push(csvCell(cells[j].innerText || ''));
28837:         }
28838: 
28839:         csv.push(line.join(','));
28840:     }
28841: 
28842:     var blob = new Blob(
28843:         ['\uFEFF' + csv.join('\r\n')],
28844:         { type: 'text/csv;charset=utf-8;' }
28845:     );
28846: 
28847:     var url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
28848:     var a = document.createElement('a');
28849: 
28850:     a.href = url;
28851:     a.download =
28852:         'rawaea-report-' +
28853:         String(_currentReport || 'report') +
28854:         '-' +
28855:         new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10) +
28856:         '.csv';
28857: 
28858:     document.body.appendChild(a);
28859:     a.click();
28860:     document.body.removeChild(a);
28861: 
28862:     URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
28863: }
28864: function _printReport() {
28865:     var resultDiv = byId('report-result');
28866: 
28867:     if (!resultDiv || !resultDiv.innerHTML) {
28868:         _showToast('لا يوجد تقرير للطباعة', 'info');
28869:         return;
28870:     }
28871: 
28872:     var printWindow = window.open('', '_blank');
28873: 
28874:     if (!printWindow) {
28875:         _showToast('الرجاء السماح بالنوافذ المنبثقة', 'warning');
28876:         return;
28877:     }
28878: 
28879:     var html =
28880:         '<!DOCTYPE html>' +
28881:         '<html dir="rtl">' +
28882:         '<head>' +
28883:         '<meta charset="UTF-8">' +
28884:         '<title>تقرير الروائع ERP</title>' +
28885:         '<link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Cairo:wght@400;700;900&display=swap" rel="stylesheet">' +
28886:         '<style>' +
28887:         'body{font-family:Cairo,sans-serif;padding:20px;color:#111827}' +
28888:         'table{width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;margin-top:15px}' +
28889:         'th,td{border:1px solid #ddd;padding:8px}' +
28890:         'th{background:#f2f2f2;font-weight:800}' +
28891:         '.text-center{text-align:center}' +
28892:         '</style>' +
28893:         '</head>' +
28894:         '<body>' +
28895:         resultDiv.innerHTML +
28896:         '<script>window.onload=function(){window.print();};<\/script>' +
28897:         '</body></html>';
28898: 
28899:     printWindow.document.open();
28900:     printWindow.document.write(html);
28901:     printWindow.document.close();
28902: }

31059:   ];

31062:   function num(v){v=Number(v);return isFinite(v)?v:0}

31064:   function date(v){return v?String(v).slice(0,10).split('-').reverse().join('/'):'-'}
31065:   function iso(v){return v?new Date(v).toISOString():null}
31066:   function toast(m,k){if(typeof showToast==='function')return showToast(m,k||'success');if(typeof Swal!=='undefined')return Swal.fire({toast:true,position:'top-end',icon:k||'success',title:m,showConfirmButton:false,timer:2600});alert(m)}
31067:   function safe(el,html){if(!el)return;if(typeof safeHTML==='function')safeHTML(el,html);else el.innerHTML=html}

31069:   function opClear(k){if(k)delete H.ops[k]}
31070:   async function actor(){var a=await supabase.auth.getUser();if(a.error||!a.data.user)throw Error('جلسة المستخدم غير صالحة');var u=await supabase.from('users').select('id,email,company_id,role,name,status,phone,employee_id,default_branch_id,active_warehouse_role').eq('auth_id',a.data.user.id).maybeSingle();if(u.error)throw u.error;if(!u.data||!u.data.id||!u.data.company_id)throw Error('تعذر تحديد سياق الموظف والشركة');H.actor=u.data;H.companyId=u.data.company_id}
31071:   async function q(view,payload){var r=await supabase.rpc('hr_query',{p_view:view,p_payload:payload||{}});if(r.error)throw r.error;if(!r.data||r.data.success===false)throw Error((r.data&&(r.data.msg||r.data.code))||'فشل قراءة HR');return r.data}
31072:   async function c(command,payload,key){var k=key||('cmd:'+command);var r=await supabase.rpc('hr_command_atomic',{p_command:command,p_payload:payload||{},p_operation_id:opKey(k),p_actor_user_id:H.actor.id,p_actor_email:H.actor.email});if(r.error)throw r.error;if(!r.data||r.data.success===false)throw Error((r.data&&(r.data.msg||r.data.code))||'فشل تنفيذ أمر HR');opClear(k);return r.data}

31077:   function table(headers,rows){if(!rows||!rows.length)return '<div class="py-10 text-center text-slate-400 font-bold">لا توجد بيانات</div>';return '<div class="overflow-auto"><table class="min-w-full text-sm"><thead><tr>'+headers.map(function(h){return '<th class="px-4 py-3 text-right bg-slate-50 text-slate-500 font-black whitespace-nowrap">'+esc(h)+'</th>'}).join('')+'</tr></thead><tbody>'+rows.join('')+'</tbody></table></div>'}

31169:   // Resilience layer: modal actions work outside the page-container, async form errors become visible, and 360 is truly read-only.
31170:   (function installModalResilience(){
31171:     document.addEventListener('click',function(e){
31172:       var ac=e.target.closest&&e.target.closest('[data-hr-action]');
31173:       if(!ac)return;
31174:       var page=E('rw-page-container');
31175:       if(page&&page.contains(ac))return;
31176:       e.preventDefault();
31177:       handle(ac.getAttribute('data-hr-action'));
31178:     },true);
31179:     window.addEventListener('unhandledrejection',function(e){
31180:       var root=E('rw-hr-modal-root');
31181:       if(!root)return;
31182:       e.preventDefault();
31183:       var msg=e.reason&&(e.reason.message||String(e.reason));
31184:       if(msg)toast(msg,'error');
31185:     });
31186:     try{
31187:       var mo=new MutationObserver(function(){
31188:         var root=E('rw-hr-modal-root');
31189:         if(!root||!E('hr360'))return;
31190:         var f=E('rw-hr-form');
31191:         if(f&&f.lastElementChild)f.lastElementChild.style.display='none';
31192:       });
31193:       mo.observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});
31194:     }catch(e){}
31195:   }());

31216:         return String(s == null ? '' : s)
31217:             .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
31218:             .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
31219:             .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
31220:             .replace(/"/g, '&quot;')

31226:     }

31229:         return Number(n || 0).toLocaleString('ar-EG') + ' EGP';
31230:     }

31233:         var d = new Date();
31234:         var local = new Date(d.getTime() - d.getTimezoneOffset() * 60000);

31236:     }

31201: // RW_CRM – إدارة علاقات العملاء (CRM)

31203: var RW_CRM = (function() {
31204:     'use strict';
31205: 
31206:     var state = {
31207:         customers: [],
31208:         assignees: [],
31209:         kpi: {},
31210:         search: '',
31211:         activeOnly: false,
31212:         searchTimer: null
31213:     };
31214: 
31215:     function _esc(s) {
31216:         return String(s == null ? '' : s)
31217:             .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
31218:             .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
31219:             .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
31220:             .replace(/"/g, '&quot;')
31221:             .replace(/'/g, '&#39;');
31222:     }
31223: 
31224:     function _fmtNum(n) {
31225:         return Number(n || 0).toLocaleString('ar-EG');
31226:     }
31227: 
31228:     function _fmtMoney(n) {
31229:         return Number(n || 0).toLocaleString('ar-EG') + ' EGP';
31230:     }
31231: 
31232:     function _today() {
31233:         var d = new Date();
31234:         var local = new Date(d.getTime() - d.getTimezoneOffset() * 60000);
31235:         return local.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
31236:     }
31237: 
31238:     function _statusLabel(s) {
31239:         var map = {
31240:             Open: 'مفتوحة',
31241:             'معلقة': 'معلقة',
31242:             completed: 'مكتملة',
31243:             'مكتملة': 'مكتملة',
31244:             cancelled: 'ملغاة',
31245:             'ملغاة': 'ملغاة'
31246:         };
31247:         return map[s] || s || 'غير محددة';
31248:     }
31249: 
31250:     function _statusClass(s) {
31251:         if (s === 'completed' || s === 'مكتملة') return 'bg-green-100 text-green-700';
31252:         if (s === 'cancelled' || s === 'ملغاة') return 'bg-gray-100 text-gray-600';
31253:         return 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-700';
31254:     }
31255: 
31256:     function _assignedLabel(customer) {
31257:         var rows = Array.isArray(customer && customer.assigned_to) ? customer.assigned_to : [];
31258:         if (!rows.length) return 'غير مسند';
31259:         var active = rows.filter(function(x) { return x && x.active !== false; });
31260:         if (!active.length) active = rows;
31261:         return active.slice(0, 2).map(function(x) {
31262:             return x.name || x.email || '—';
31263:         }).join('، ') + (active.length > 2 ? ' +' + (active.length - 2) : '');
31264:     }
31265: 
31266:     async function _loadDirectory() {
31267:         var res = await supabase.rpc('crm_customer_directory', {
31268:             p_search: state.search || null,
31269:             p_active_only: state.activeOnly,
31270:             p_limit: 200,
31271:             p_offset: 0
31272:         });
31273:         if (res.error) throw res.error;
31274: 
31275:         var payload = res.data || {};
31276:         state.customers = Array.isArray(payload.customers) ? payload.customers : [];
31277:         state.assignees = Array.isArray(payload.assignees) ? payload.assignees : [];
31278:         state.kpi = payload.kpi || {};
31279:         return payload;
31280:     }
31281: 
31282:     function _renderKpis() {
31283:         var k = state.kpi || {};
31284:         return '<div class="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-3 xl:grid-cols-6 gap-4">' +
31285:             '<div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border p-4"><div class="text-xs text-gray-500">إجمالي العملاء</div><div class="text-2xl font-black text-indigo-600 mt-2">' + _fmtNum(k.total_customers) + '</div></div>' +
31286:             '<div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border p-4"><div class="text-xs text-gray-500">عملاء نشطون</div><div class="text-2xl font-black text-green-600 mt-2">' + _fmtNum(k.active_customers) + '</div></div>' +
31287:             '<div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border p-4"><div class="text-xs text-gray-500">ذمم مسجلة</div><div class="text-2xl font-black text-red-600 mt-2">' + _fmtMoney(k.master_debt_total) + '</div></div>' +
31288:             '<div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border p-4"><div class="text-xs text-gray-500">متابعات مفتوحة</div><div class="text-2xl font-black text-amber-600 mt-2">' + _fmtNum(k.open_followups) + '</div></div>' +
31289:             '<div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border p-4"><div class="text-xs text-gray-500">متأخرة</div><div class="text-2xl font-black text-rose-600 mt-2">' + _fmtNum(k.overdue_followups) + '</div></div>' +
31290:             '<div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border p-4"><div class="text-xs text-gray-500">مستحقة اليوم</div><div class="text-2xl font-black text-blue-600 mt-2">' + _fmtNum(k.due_today) + '</div></div>' +
31291:         '</div>';
31292:     }
31293: 
31294:     function _renderTable() {
31295:         if (!state.customers.length) {
31296:             return '<div class="text-center py-16 text-gray-400"><div class="text-5xl mb-3">👥</div><div class="font-black text-lg">لا توجد عملاء مطابقون</div><div class="text-sm mt-2">غيّر البحث أو الفلاتر ثم أعد المحاولة.</div></div>';
31297:         }
31298: 
31299:         var html = '<div class="overflow-x-auto"><table class="w-full text-sm">' +
31300:             '<thead class="bg-slate-50"><tr>' +
31301:             '<th class="p-3 text-right">العميل</th>' +
31302:             '<th class="p-3 text-right">التواصل</th>' +
31303:             '<th class="p-3 text-right">التصنيف</th>' +
31304:             '<th class="p-3 text-center">المبيعات</th>' +
31305:             '<th class="p-3 text-center">الأوردرات</th>' +
31306:             '<th class="p-3 text-center">المتابعة القادمة</th>' +
31307:             '<th class="p-3 text-right">المسؤول</th>' +
31308:             '<th class="p-3 text-center">الإجراء</th>' +
31309:             '</tr></thead><tbody>';
31310: 
31311:         for (var i = 0; i < state.customers.length; i++) {
31312:             var c = state.customers[i] || {};
31313:             var overdue = Number(c.overdue_followups || 0) > 0;
31314:             var next = c.next_followup_date ? String(c.next_followup_date) : '—';
31315: 
31316:             html += '<tr class="border-b hover:bg-slate-50">' +
31317:                 '<td class="p-3"><div class="font-black">' + _esc(c.name) + '</div><div class="text-xs text-gray-400">' + _esc(c.customer_code) + '</div></td>' +
31318:                 '<td class="p-3"><div>' + _esc(c.phone || '—') + '</div><div class="text-xs text-gray-400">' + _esc(c.area || '—') + '</div></td>' +
31319:                 '<td class="p-3"><div class="font-bold">' + _esc(c.customer_type || '—') + '</div><div class="text-xs text-gray-400">' + _esc(c.payment_type || '—') + '</div></td>' +
31320:                 '<td class="p-3 text-center font-black">' + _fmtMoney(c.sales_total) + '</td>' +
31321:                 '<td class="p-3 text-center font-black">' + _fmtNum(c.order_count) + '</td>' +
31322:                 '<td class="p-3 text-center"><span class="px-2 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-black ' + (overdue ? 'bg-rose-100 text-rose-700' : 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-700') + '">' + _esc(next) + '</span></td>' +
31323:                 '<td class="p-3">' + _esc(_assignedLabel(c)) + '</td>' +
31324:                 '<td class="p-3 text-center"><button type="button" data-crm-open360="' + _esc(c.id) + '" class="px-4 py-2 bg-indigo-600 text-white rounded-xl font-black">متابعة</button></td>' +
31325:             '</tr>';
31326:         }
31327: 
31328:         return html + '</tbody></table></div>';
31329:     }
31330: 
31331:     function _bindDirectory() {
31332:         var search = byId('crm-search');
31333:         if (search) {
31334:             search.value = state.search;
31335:             search.addEventListener('input', function() {
31336:                 state.search = search.value.trim();
31337:                 clearTimeout(state.searchTimer);
31338:                 state.searchTimer = setTimeout(function() {
31339:                     _loadDirectory().then(function() {
31340:                         safeHTML(byId('crm-customers-list'), _renderTable());
31341:                         _bindOpen360();
31342:                         safeHTML(byId('crm-kpis'), _renderKpis());
31343:                     }).catch(function(e) {
31344:                         showToast(e.message || 'فشل البحث', 'error');
31345:                     });
31346:                 }, 250);
31347:             });
31348:         }

31123:   async function loadPeople(){var d=await q('employees');H.employees=d.rows||[];return H.employees}

31169:   // Resilience layer: modal actions work outside the page-container, async form errors become visible, and 360 is truly read-only.
31170:   (function installModalResilience(){
31171:     document.addEventListener('click',function(e){
31172:       var ac=e.target.closest&&e.target.closest('[data-hr-action]');
31173:       if(!ac)return;
31174:       var page=E('rw-page-container');
31175:       if(page&&page.contains(ac))return;
31176:       e.preventDefault();
31177:       handle(ac.getAttribute('data-hr-action'));
31178:     },true);
31179:     window.addEventListener('unhandledrejection',function(e){
31180:       var root=E('rw-hr-modal-root');
31181:       if(!root)return;
31182:       e.preventDefault();
31183:       var msg=e.reason&&(e.reason.message||String(e.reason));
31184:       if(msg)toast(msg,'error');
31185:     });
31186:     try{
31187:       var mo=new MutationObserver(function(){
31188:         var root=E('rw-hr-modal-root');
31189:         if(!root||!E('hr360'))return;
31190:         var f=E('rw-hr-form');
31191:         if(f&&f.lastElementChild)f.lastElementChild.style.display='none';
31192:       });
31193:       mo.observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});
31194:     }catch(e){}
31195:   }());
